## Heuristic / Inference Reanalysis And Validation

- Standalone file versus fold: standalone wins. The proposed source tree names the file, the file page is the established root for seven children, `LoginDialogPane` documents it as separate, MainMenu constructs a complete `ChangePasswordDialogPane` type, and the packet helpers are feature-specific. A fold would now create unnecessary cross-file ownership and header ambiguity.
- Class layout: RTTI directly proves `ChangePasswordDialogPane : DialogPane, Singleton<ChangePasswordDialogPane>`. The Singleton base PMD has `mdisp 0x26c`, the two secondary COLs have offsets `0xa0` and `0xa4`, callers allocate `0x26c`, and no additional member storage follows the 620-byte `DialogPane` base. A fieldless derived declaration is therefore the narrowest source shape.
- Constructor publication: the apparent writes at `0x004fdd96/0x004fdd9d` are compiler/template lowering for the direct Singleton base. Handwriting `g_pChangePasswordDialogPane = this` would duplicate base behavior and is rejected.
- Teardown: `0x005023a0` is a constructor-unwind singleton clear; `0x00502474` and `0x0050247f` are adjustor thunks; `0x00502650` is a scalar deleting destructor wrapper that clears the Singleton and invokes `DialogPane` destruction. An implicit virtual derived destructor generated from the bases explains all four islands. A handwritten destructor body is rejected.
- Core naming: base vtable slots and analogs support `OnControlCommand`, `UpdateActionButton`, and `HandlePacketEvent`. The sole command caller and semantics support private `SubmitPasswordChange`.
- Constructor APIs: established `LoginDialogPane`, `NewUserDialogPane`, `BackStoryDialogPane`, `DialogPane.h`, and control classes resolve `InitRectBounds`, `AddControl`, control constructors, focus/default methods, resource binding, `OnCreate`, `OnShow`, and slide helpers.
- Packet children: dated direct xrefs prove UID0002QC active at `0x004fe778` and UID0002QD active at `0x004fe551`; exhaustive negative searches preserve UID0002QB and UID0002QE as retained siblings. Their behavior and ownership remain accepted; the ordinary callback applied the exact API/decode formal repairs below and Gate 2A verified the generated result.
- Singleton bytes: the dated 2026-08-13 bounded read returned `00 00 00 00`, resolving the old-session conflict for this recommendation without claiming permanent IDB authority. A null definition is source-idiomatic and byte-faithful; the historical FF sessions remain provenance only.
- Header necessity: MainMenu code constructs this concrete class, packet children define member methods, and the source must include a complete declaration before those definitions. The target H requires `DialogPane.h`, `Singleton.h`, an Event forward declaration, the singleton extern, and the derived declaration; the target CPP additionally requires the exact owner headers and C/Win32 declarations listed in the formal include manifest.

```cpp
void ChangePasswordDialogPane::OnControlCommand(
    int controlIndex,
    int notifyCode)
{
    (void)notifyCode;

    if (controlIndex == 1)
    {
        SubmitPasswordChange();
    }
    else if (controlIndex == 2)
    {
        SlideCloseVertical();
        CloseDialog();
    }
}

void ChangePasswordDialogPane::UpdateActionButton()
{
    ImageButtonControlPane *actionButton =
        GetChild<ImageButtonControlPane>(1);

    if (GetChild<TextEditControlPane>(3)->IsEmpty() ||
        GetChild<TextEditControlPane>(4)->IsEmpty() ||
        GetChild<TextEditControlPane>(5)->IsEmpty() ||
        GetChild<TextEditControlPane>(6)->IsEmpty())
    {
        actionButton->Disable();
    }
    else
    {
        actionButton->Enable();
    }
}

bool ChangePasswordDialogPane::HandlePacketEvent(Event *event)
{
    const unsigned char *packet =
        static_cast<const unsigned char *>(event->m_payload.m_packet.m_data);
    if (packet[0] != 2)
        return false;

    return HandlePasswordChangeReply(packet);
}

void ChangePasswordDialogPane::SubmitPasswordChange()
{
    wchar_t accountName[16];
    wchar_t currentPassword[16];
    wchar_t newPassword[16];
    wchar_t passwordConfirmation[16];

    GetChild<TextEditControlPane>(3)->ReadText(accountName, 15);
    GetChild<TextEditControlPane>(4)->ReadText(currentPassword, 15);
    GetChild<TextEditControlPane>(5)->ReadText(newPassword, 15);
    GetChild<TextEditControlPane>(6)->ReadText(passwordConfirmation, 15);

    bool containsDigit = false;
    for (const wchar_t *cursor = newPassword; *cursor != L'\0'; ++cursor)
    {
        if (*cursor >= L'0' && *cursor <= L'9')
        {
            containsDigit = true;
            break;
        }
    }

    if (!containsDigit)
    {
        SetHoverControl(5);
        new AlertPane(
            L"password must containt at least 1 number",
            this,
            L"OK",
            NULL);
        return;
    }

    if (wcscmp(newPassword, passwordConfirmation) != 0)
    {
        SetHoverControl(5);
        new AlertPane(
            g_pLanguageMan->GetLocalizedString(90),
            this,
            L"OK",
            NULL);
        return;
    }

    new ScreenDimmer(5, g_pScreenPane);
    SendPasswordChangeRequest(accountName, currentPassword, newPassword);
}
```

UID0002QD exact replacement `RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE`:

```cpp
namespace
{
bool IsPasswordChangeAccountNameReplyError(unsigned char result)
{
    switch (result) {
    case 3:
    case 4:
    case 14:
    case 16:
    case 17:
    case 18:
    case 20:
    case 21:
        return true;

    default:
        return false;
    }
}

bool IsPasswordChangeCurrentPasswordReplyError(unsigned char result)
{
    return result == 15;
}

bool IsPasswordChangeNewPasswordReplyError(unsigned char result)
{
    switch (result) {
    case 5:
    case 6:
    case 7:
    case 8:
    case 9:
        return true;

    default:
        return false;
    }
}
}

bool ChangePasswordDialogPane::HandlePasswordChangeReply(const unsigned char *packet)
{
    if (g_pScreenDimmer != NULL) {
        delete g_pScreenDimmer;
    }

    const unsigned char result = packet[1];
    const unsigned char messageLength = packet[2];
    char narrowMessage[0x100];
    wchar_t message[0x100];

    memmove(narrowMessage, packet + 3, messageLength);
    narrowMessage[messageLength] = 0;
    const int wideLength = MultiByteToWideChar(
        CP_ACP, 0, narrowMessage, messageLength, message, 0x100);
    if (wideLength >= 0x100)
        __report_rangecheckfailure();
    message[static_cast<unsigned char>(wideLength)] = L'\0';

    if (result != 0) {
        if (IsPasswordChangeAccountNameReplyError(result)) {
            SetHoverControl(3);
        } else if (IsPasswordChangeCurrentPasswordReplyError(result)) {
            SetHoverControl(4);
        } else if (IsPasswordChangeNewPasswordReplyError(result)) {
            SetHoverControl(5);
        }

        if (message[0] != L'\0')
            new AlertPane(message, this, L"OK", NULL);
        return true;
    }

    if (message[0] != L'\0')
        new AlertPane(message, this, L"OK", NULL);
    SlideCloseVertical();
    CloseDialog();
    return true;
}
```

- Localization method: the generated owner header physically declares `LanguageMan::GetLocalizedString(int) const` and does not declare `GetString`. Use `GetLocalizedString(90)`; index 90 remains binary-proven.
- Button-state method name: exact base vtable slot and DialogPane H name it `UpdateActionButton`; use that rather than the old `ValidateInputFields` description.
